# SZ_IJK - families I (book-level recent performance), J (combinations / learned tree), K (P7) of T-SIZER-EXHAUST

**First-order tables: ROLL-EXCL basis (33 roll-artifact trades excluded). Exact finalists table: ROLL-FIXED (engine ROLLFIX1 roll months re-run for F03, F08, J01-J04, JZ01-04; reference = base_b_rollfix S1-COMB).** **EVERY NUMBER IN THE FAMILY TABLES BELOW IS FIRST-ORDER SCREEN ONLY. Only the finalists table at the end carries EXACT engine numbers (exact L6M done for F01-F12; exact PRE / X' / HOLD running for 6 of them). First-order overstates the cuts: exact L6M equal-mean cut of the best pair is +33.5% vs +71% first-order; the K caps +9 to +13% vs +35%.**

Updated 2026-11-10 07:58Z by SZ-IJK. Base S1b-COMB (controls NOCUT/UNIF on finalists), first-order (pnl per planned lot x new lots, fee 4.84/contract RT re-applied), down-only multipliers {0,.5,1} unless labelled; PRE-PROOF, post-hoc, shadow only, no PASS claims. Scored with SZ-CORE sz_core.Z.score: windows as of 2026-11-09, maxDD/worst-20/Ulcer on ALL weeks, carrying week (largest |cell-ref| weekly delta) dropped from MEANS only. Superseded earlier IJK numbers that dropped carry weeks before maxDD. Reference = Q1-A live lots (base). Ledger /mnt/l2/l2vast/_th_sz/ledger_IJK.parquet (1837 cells). Prereg sha line in DECISIONS.md (autopilot/SZ_IJK_PREREG.md).

Objectives: (a) maxDD cut and d mean $/wk at equal lots vs Q1-A; (b) every cell scaled by one global factor so its L6M mean equals Q1-A's, then maxDD cut. Placebo = same-count multipliers permuted within book x session (100 draws, causal); family p = max-stat over the cells of the same set (own-draw p = placebo p; not a kill on its own). WF = purged monthly (26 wk, 7 d embargo) pick by equal-mean train maxDD. Live week = frozen file, first-order, state rebuilt from base history plus live trades.

## Family I

Family I: the best equal-mean cell (I5 stack rolling Calmar {D: 20, mult: 0.0, x: 3.0}) cuts L6M maxDD 49% at the same mean (PRE 26%, X' 53%), at equal lots it cuts 68% and costs -8191 $/wk; placebo p 0.05 (eq), family p 1.00 (max-stat), WF fail, live-week delta -104810, label no. 10 of 186 cells cut L6M and PRE maxDD without lowering the L6M mean (dominate Q1-A on both). Best dominator: I3 book rolling Calmar {D: 10, mult: 0.5, x: 1.0} (cut 21% / 37% / 47% / 44% on L6M/HOLD/PRE/X', d mean +349, placebo eq 0.10, live -38365). Among the trade-off cells the median is 4.6 pp of cut per $1k/wk of mean given up.

## Family J

J pairs (min and product of the best cells of every pool, own I/K/tree and the other owners M_*.npz; pairs of already-selected cells: selection inflated, whole-set family null is SZ-CORE's): the best equal-mean cell (J pair min {a: I:83d7d23f2f5d, b: ext-VT:c5f9e199982a}) cuts L6M maxDD 99% at the same mean (PRE 53%, X' 58%), at equal lots it cuts 99% and costs -7726 $/wk; placebo p 0.03 (eq), family p 1.00 (max-stat), WF fail, live-week delta n/a, label REFERENCE-ONLY (turn-off / skip row, standing rule: layers stay on) LEAD (DD at equal mean; nulls/WF not all met) FAILS-LIVE-WEEK. 336 of 1228 cells cut L6M and PRE maxDD without lowering the L6M mean (dominate Q1-A on both). Best dominator: J pair min {a: ext-K:d16e6b6b3c26, b: ext-VT:e3f124a36d05} (cut 93% / 38% / 65% / 73% on L6M/HOLD/PRE/X', d mean +1401, placebo eq 0.02, live n/a). Among the trade-off cells the median is 22.1 pp of cut per $1k/wk of mean given up.

J learned monotone tree (<=8 leaves, purged monthly WF from 2023-08, 3 seeds x 3 threshold pairs, label-shuffle null): the best equal-mean cell (J-tree monotone <=8 leaves WF {seed: 1, thr: [-0.1, 0.05]}) cuts L6M maxDD 36% at the same mean (PRE 24%, X' 11%), at equal lots it cuts 37% and costs -553 $/wk; placebo p 0.16 (eq), family p 0.68 (max-stat), WF ok, live-week delta -26693, label REFERENCE-ONLY (turn-off / skip row, standing rule: layers stay on) LEAD (DD at equal mean; nulls/WF not all met) FAILS-LIVE-WEEK. 1 of 9 cells cut L6M and PRE maxDD without lowering the L6M mean (dominate Q1-A on both). Best dominator: J-tree monotone <=8 leaves WF {seed: 1, thr: [-0.15, 0.0]} (cut 3% / -0% / 9% / 8% on L6M/HOLD/PRE/X', d mean +240, placebo eq 0.35, live +0). Among the trade-off cells the median is 55.4 pp of cut per $1k/wk of mean given up.

J reference rows (sizer off / old ladder L / B up-only / ZL-1 vol-target on Q1-A): the best equal-mean cell (REF vol-target ZL-1 (down-only on Q1-A) {}) cuts L6M maxDD 16% at the same mean (PRE 22%, X' 50%), at equal lots it cuts 13% and costs +705 $/wk; placebo p n/a (eq), family p n/a (max-stat), WF fail, live-week delta n/a, label reference row. 1 of 1 cells cut L6M and PRE maxDD without lowering the L6M mean (dominate Q1-A on both). ## Family K

Family K: the best equal-mean cell (K6 ext-VT on P7 only {cell: c3eb2c6eff46, orig: ext ext-VT {\cell\: \c3eb2c6eff46\}}) cuts L6M maxDD 63% at the same mean (PRE 50%, X' 67%), at equal lots it cuts 57% and costs +3477 $/wk; placebo p 0.01 (eq), family p 0.64 (max-stat), WF fail, live-week delta n/a, label REFERENCE-ONLY (turn-off / skip row, standing rule: layers stay on) LEAD (DD at equal mean; nulls/WF not all met). 148 of 408 cells cut L6M and PRE maxDD without lowering the L6M mean (dominate Q1-A on both). Best dominator: K6 ext-VT on P7 only {cell: c3eb2c6eff46, orig: ext ext-VT {\cell\: \c3eb2c6eff46\}} (cut 57% / 38% / 42% / 62% on L6M/HOLD/PRE/X', d mean +3477, placebo eq 0.01, live n/a). Among the trade-off cells the median is 9.5 pp of cut per $1k/wk of mean given up.
